- **2026-06-10 — NDA Physics /notes: 7 chapters shipped (Optics, Laws of Motion, Heat/Thermo, Modern Physics, Kinematics, Fluids, Work-Energy-Power) — formula-heavy + diagram-rich, completing NDA Physics to 9 of 14 chapters.** Per-chapter one-liners in [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md). **272 q · ~124 concepts · 25 static SVG diagrams**, all reorder-only + quiz-ready per Step 1b. NDA Physics notes now cover 9 of 14 chapters (only Gravitation/Units/Oscillations — 3 borderline — and Astronomy/Energy-Sources — below the gate — remain).
  - **Profile INVERTS Chemistry's.** Where NDA Chemistry was recall→reference-table, Physics is **formula-variant the workhorse** (every concept a `formula.latex` + an arithmetic-verified `authoredExample`) + **diagram-rich** — physics IS the high-hit-rate case of [[notes-diagram-decision-rule]] (optics = ray diagrams, mechanics = free-body diagrams, kinematics = v–t/x–t graphs, fluids = buoyancy/pressure). So diagrams were built INLINE with the chapters (not deferred): 25 across the 7, vs the recall chapters' near-zero.
  - **Orchestration — agents build their own SVGs, main agent wires serially.** The 7 parallel agents each authored their `_data` AND built their own static-SVG diagram components (isolated files — this distributes the heavy SVG work that bottlenecked the Chemistry diagram pass on me); each returned a handoff with a **diagram wiring manifest** (slug + component + import + case + union line). The main agent did the serial merge of the SHARED files only — `chapters.ts` (7 entries), the `VisualizationSlug` union (+25), `ConceptUnitCard` `renderVisualization` (+25 imports/cases), and one combined **272-row tag INSERT**. Agents were told NOT to touch the 3 shared files.
  - **Survived a mid-flight account session-limit wipe.** The FIRST 7-agent fan-out was killed mid-work by the account session limit (resets 5pm) — all 7 returned no usable handoff, 2 had partial untracked files. Clean recovery (nothing committed, no DB writes, removed the partials → `main` intact). After the reset, a single **canary agent (Work-Energy-Power)** confirmed the limit was back before re-fanning the other 6 — cheap probe vs re-wasting 7 spawns. Lesson banked: a 7-agent fan-out is the most exposed to a mid-flight limit; the clean-recovery design (untracked + no DB writes until the serial merge) is what made it damage-free, and a canary is the right re-probe.
  - **One agent's handoff truncated → read-only recovery agent.** The Laws-of-Motion agent's final message was truncated in the notification (lost the 41-row tag INSERT + diagram manifest); `SendMessage` isn't an available tool and the transcript file isn't Grep-able. Fix: a **read-only recovery agent** re-derived the tag INSERT + manifest from the on-disk `_data` files + the bank. Reusable pattern when an agent's output is lost but its files landed.
  - **The mistag detector + notes-lint earned their place (5 errors caught, all fixed).** Three questions were filed in a DB subtopic that didn't match the concept the agent put them under: a Raman-scattering Q misfiled under "Atomic Structure" (→ a `mod-quantum-and-modern-em` concept) and 2 distance/speed-basics Qs filed under "Equations of Motion and Graphs" (→ `kin-foundations` concepts). These surfaced as notes-lint "pyqExampleId in a different subtopic" + "unknown concept_slug" errors AND in the mistag detector (a notes subtopic spanning >1 DB subtopic). Fixed by **DB reclassification of the 3 questions** to the subtopic matching their concept (no subtopic *rename* → no guide-sync) + retag. One residual benign mistag accepted (2 latent-heat Qs DB-filed under Calorimetry but grouped under Phase-Change as drills — pedagogically sound, doesn't error). Also: one imaged TIR featured-PYQ swapped to a non-imaged one (worked-example card can't render figures); one lint apostrophe (a `F'` prime mark in an agent SVG → unicode `′`) caught by the gate + fixed; one Heat worked-example/PYQ number-dup (notes-lint #5) rewritten.

- **2026-06-10 — NDA Chemistry /notes: first Chemistry subject shipped (7 chapters, 250 of 262 q) + Quiz-Factory learnings folded into the notes editorial workflow.** A pilot-then-batch build that also closed the loop the user asked for — author notes quiz-ready up front so the later harvest needs no rework. Per-chapter one-liners in [ARCHITECTURE.md](ARCHITECTURE.md); lessons in [[notes-self-sufficient-template]] + [[quiz-formula-coverage-gap]].
  - **The workflow update (the durable artifact, done FIRST):** CLAUDE.md "Notes editorial workflow" gained **Step 1b — Author for clean quiz-harvest** + `quiz:coverage`/`quiz:lint` in Step 4. The rules (each tied to a real Quiz-Factory rework): fill EVERY reference-table cell (a `—` → broken `fact` distractor, the HP Vitamin-K precedent); put every recallable formula in `formula.latex`, not prose-only (but don't pad techniques); self-contained `selfCheckExample`/`practiceSet` stems (they harvest into *shuffled* atoms, so no back-references); standalone-meaningful `concept.name`; ≥12 self-contained `traps`. So building a notes chapter now bakes in quiz-readiness for free instead of forcing a harvest→re-author→delete+re-assemble cycle later.
  - **Scope + template fit:** Tier-1 (6 chapters that clear the bank-coverage gate) + a compact Mole Concept (9 q, the one numeric/Calculate chapter). NDA Chemistry is recall-heavy → the **reference-variant is the workhorse** (the NDA Biology Human Physiology profile), so most concepts are filled lookup tables, not formulas. **All 7 reorder-only** — the DB subtopics were already coherent teaching buckets AND the shipped `/guide` references them, so friendly display titles + `subtopicOrder` sufficed (no Phase-D, no [[shipped-chapter-rename-downstream-sync]] cost). **Diagrams deferred** — chemistry is mostly non-spatial; the one real diagram opportunity (allotrope structures) is batched for later per the selective [[notes-diagram-decision-rule]].
  - **Process — pilot then parallel batch:** Carbon built solo first (45 q · 15 concepts) to stand up the `nda-chemistry` subject infra (`/notes/nda-chemistry/page.tsx`, registry-derived) + validate the recall + quiz-ready profile + fact-accuracy bar; shipped green. Then the **other 6 chapters via 6 concurrent sub-agents** (one per chapter, unique slug prefix `atom-`/`acid-`/`matt-`/`rxn-`/`ind-`/`mole-`, each authoring ONLY its own `_data/*` + wrappers off the Carbon template + the Step-1b rules, returning a structured handoff) + a **serial merge** by the main agent of the shared files (`chapters.ts` 6 imports+entries, one combined 165-row tag INSERT) — the same orchestration as the 2026-06-09 NDA Maths parallel batch. **80 concepts across the 6 chapters, 165/165 q tagged at 100% coverage.**
  - **Gates:** typecheck clean; a **mistag detector** (group tags by `subtopic_slug`, assert each maps to exactly ONE DB subtopic) found 0 cross-subtopic errors; **notes-lint #5 caught 2 worked-examples that re-used their featured PYQ's numbers** (Atomic Structure — Na₂CO₃=106 + the oxygen-isotope average) → rewritten to distinct problems (CaCO₃=100, Cl 35/37→35.5); `quiz:coverage` ✓ on all 7 (0 formula gaps; Mole's 10 traps < 12 is by-design for a 9-q chapter); notes:order synced 28 subtopics; full prepush PASS both pushes.
  - **Stealth issues:** only **one** flagged across all 165 re-derived keys — Atomic Structure `58dfcfd7` (isoelectronic-pairs item with TWO valid options B + D; stored key D is correct, so a flawed-question flag, NOT a wrong-key) — logged for a future source review, no DB change. Unlike prior notes builds on "audit-closed" chapters (which kept surfacing stealth wrong-keys), NDA Chemistry's audit-closed bank held up clean — consistent with [[audit-effort-by-question-type]] (recall/fact questions are ~0% wrong-key).
  - **Both open items then completed the same day (2026-06-10):** (a) the **4 Tier-2 thin chapters** — Metals & Non-Metals (17 q) · Hydrogen & Water (11) · Chemical Bonding (11) · Chemistry in Everyday Life (10) = 30 concepts, 49 q, all reorder-only + quiz-ready, built via a **second 4-agent parallel batch** + serial merge; and (b) the **4 deferred diagrams** — allotrope structures (Carbon), Bohr shells + periodic-trends (Atomic Structure), states-of-matter (Matter), all static SVGs wired into the `VisualizationSlug` union + `ConceptUnitCard` `renderVisualization` + the 4 host concepts (diagrams `visualizations/*.tsx` 120 → 124). **Only Practical Chemistry (3 q) stays deferred** (below the bank-coverage gate). NDA Chemistry notes now cover **259 of 262 q (~99%) across 11 of 12 chapters** (~125 concepts). Process notes: the mistag detector + notes-lint #5 stayed clean; one Tier-2 agent transcribed a PYQ UUID with the wrong 12-hex tail (it collided with a sibling question's) — caught by a DB re-query before the INSERT, a reminder to verify agent-returned UUIDs against the bank rather than trust the transcription. Two soft notes-lint warns remain (an un-tagged foundation concept in Metals + a structure/H-bonding teaching concept in Hydrogen, neither with a dedicated bank PYQ — the documented "soft warn for un-tagged concepts" allowance). **Diagram expansion (same day, prompted by the user asking "shouldn't chemistry have more diagrams?"):** added **8 more static SVGs (124 → 132)** on the genuinely *spatial-answer* concepts — soap micelle (the heads-out/tails-in orientation trap), pH-scale strip, distillation apparatus, Tyndall/colloid, ionic-vs-covalent electron transfer vs sharing, water/ice H-bonding lattice (why ice floats), functional-group structures, reaction-type shapes. The discriminator applied was **"is the QUESTION spatial?" not "does the topic sound spatial?"** — the name↔formula recall tables (washing soda, alloys, fertilizers, drugs) stay table-only because a diagram there is decorative. NDA Chemistry now carries **12 diagrams (~7% of concepts)** — richer than a pure-recall chapter, far leaner than a geometry chapter. Reinforces [[notes-diagram-decision-rule]]: the trigger is the spatial *answer/trap*, not the subject's visual-sounding name.

- **2026-06-10 — `errorTransforms` distractor-candidate generator + NDA Maths Quiz Wave 2 (3 chapters, all themes) + the per-chapter notes-enrichment CADENCE codified.** Three things from one long session (per-chapter detail in [QUIZ_FACTORY.md](QUIZ_FACTORY.md) "Chapter status" + [[daily-quiz-pilot]]):
  - **`errorTransforms()`** ([src/lib/quiz/atoms.ts](src/lib/quiz/atoms.ts), 8 TDD): `harvestProblem` now seeds `candidate_distractors` for SIMPLE-numeric answers (int/decimal/`\frac{a}{b}`/`a/b`) with mechanical wrong-variants (sign flip, double, off-by-one, fraction swap; `8`→`[-8,16,9]`), falling back to siblings for non-numeric — cuts cold distractor authoring. A PROPOSAL the verify pass accepts/edits, never auto-accepted. **Honest ceiling: numeric-only**, so it helped little on the vector/expression-heavy Wave-2 chapters (verified in practice — the agents authored mostly fresh).
  - **Wave 2 COMPLETE** — 3D-Geometry (11 quizzes), Sequence-Series (11), Differentiation (8) = **30 quizzes** across computation + formula + traps, built via parallel agents (computation subtopic-split 2–3/chapter; 1 formula agent — no notes edit, all 3 chapters already had ≥12 formula atoms; 1 trap agent). Re-derivation during authoring caught + fixed a real notes error (Seq-Series `S_n=5n-2n²`→a₃=−5, the method had miscomputed S₂).
  - **The CADENCE** (the durable artifact, QUIZ_FACTORY "Recipe"): per-chapter build = harvest → **`quiz:coverage` (completeness gate)** → computation (re-derivation = correctness check) → formula author → **formula ENRICHMENT (notes edit, conditional — triage flags first)** → **trap CALLOUTS (notes edit, conditional)** → assemble → gate. **Building a chapter is now WHEN its /notes get enriched** — Wave 2 added **25 misconception callouts** across the 3 chapters (students gained "common mistakes" sections). Trap-atom keys are predictable (`<concept>:trap:<existing-count+pos>`; `quiz:verify` fail-fast catches a mispredict — all matched). Deferred (optional): Bucket 2 = enrich the empty-`formula.latex` concepts (completeness only; chapters already have formula quizzes).

- **2026-06-10 — Matrices & Determinants property quizzes + clean formula/property theme split (Path A + B).** The chapter is rule-dense but had ~1 property atom — the rules lived in `definition` prose (unharvested) or were defaulted to `formula`. **Path A** (additive, 0 churn): enriched ~10 concepts' `formula.latex` with 25 rule-identities themed `property` → 2 property quizzes; the **360 caught a single→bundle flip** (transpose/symmetric appends would flip their existing `auto` atom to needs_review and dirty the shipped formula quizzes) → reverted those 2, sync `0-stale`. **Path B** (user-approved one-time cleanup): re-themed the 12 rule-formulas that sat in `formula` only by accident (det(AB), det(kA), (AB)⁻¹, adjoint/transpose/orthogonal/diagonal/rotation rules) → `property`, moved them `-formulas.ts`→`-properties.ts`, deleted + re-assembled the 3 affected drafts → clean split: **formula = construction-only (1 quiz: det 2×2, A⁻¹=adj/|A|, Cramer's, cofactor expansion, roots-of-unity); property = ALL rules (3 quizzes, 38 atoms)**. `quiz integrity` 0; no notes/atom-content change in Path B (re-theme only). Matrices now 16 quizzes. **Durable lesson ([[quiz-formula-coverage-gap]], sharpened): an atom's `theme` is FIRST-PASS metadata — classify it correctly when authoring (a det rule → `property`); defaulting everything to one theme then re-theming later is pure churn (the Path A enrich + Path B re-theme WAS that cost).** A per-concept `recallTheme` notes hint to fix it structurally was judged low-ROI (Matrices was the one strongly property-dense chapter; bundles + fuzzy cases make a hint imperfect).

- **2026-06-10 — NDA Maths daily-quiz expansion (5→7 fully-themed chapters) + the theme-coverage learning + the `quiz:coverage` guardrail + the cross-project learning-propagation protocol.** A long session; operational detail in [QUIZ_FACTORY.md](QUIZ_FACTORY.md) + [[daily-quiz-pilot]]. Durable parts:
  - **Wave 1 (prioritized-subset campaign):** parallel sub-agents author distractors — one per chapter, heavy chapters subtopic-split; the main agent harvests/syncs/verifies/assembles/pushes serially (DB + cross-app push can't fan out). Functions (4 quizzes), Lines (7), Trigonometric Identities (9) — every harvestable theme. Atom-key predictions all matched on `quiz:verify` (fail-fast catches misses).
  - **The theme-coverage learning (the durable one — [[quiz-formula-coverage-gap]]):** EVERY quiz theme has a notes-source dependency — `formula`←`formula.latex`, `trap`←`traps` callouts, `fact`←reference tables — EXCEPT `property`, which has no source (hand-tagged at verify). So **"ready atoms consumed" ≠ "theme complete"**, and nothing flagged the gap. Surfaced by the user asking "did you cover all the formulas?" — it had NOT: Trig-Id's `formula.latex` carried 15 of ~26 (half-angle, sin2A/tan2A, tan3A, 2-of-4 product-to-sum + sum-to-product all taught in prose, never exposed). Fix: enrich `formula.latex` **append-only** (preserves piece indices + sha1 fingerprints, so already-verified pieces survive re-sync) → re-harvest → author only the new pieces → delete + re-assemble the frozen-snapshot quiz. Lines had ZERO formula.latex + ZERO traps despite being formula/trap-rich.
  - **`quiz:coverage` probe (the guardrail):** `npm run quiz:coverage [route] [chapter] [--review]` (`src/lib/quiz/coverage.ts`, 10 TDD cases) flags concepts that teach a formula in prose but leave `formula.latex` EMPTY (strong) + chapters with <12 `traps` (no Common-Traps quiz). **Chapter-wide matching + dedupe** so a formula restated across concepts isn't a false gap — the design fix after v1 over-flagged Vectors. Triage aid, not a gate. Validated: Vectors/Binomial read clean (= the manual audit); Lines went from would-flag-all-11 → 1 borderline.
  - **The revisit (existing-work, permission-gated):** with the user's go-ahead per step, audited + closed the already-"complete" chapters — Functions (traps + formula), Lines (formula + traps from scratch), Matrices (9 trap callouts); Stats/Prob/Vectors formula audit came back CLEAN (field-count DID equal completeness there). Also fixed a notes ERROR the quiz build re-derived: Lines `(1,-2)`→`(1,-1)`.
  - **Learning-propagation protocol ([[learning-propagation-protocol]]):** user-mandated working agreement — fold a new learning into NEW work via a durable artifact (automatic); for EXISTING/shipped work, log a backfill candidate (`SUGGESTIONS.md` "Backfill ledger") + a fixed 360 analysis (scope · blast radius · really-applies · risk/reversibility · cost · recommendation) + EXPLICIT permission before reworking. **Promoted to the global `~/.claude/CLAUDE.md`** ("Applying New Learnings" section) so it governs every project; per-project ledgers stay local.

Process notes: Binomial Theorem tripped 3 notes-lint #5 worked-example/PYQ-duplication warnings (I seeded examples from the featured PYQ) — all rewritten to distinct problems before commit, so the remaining 4 chapters were authored example-first; parallel git-push (background) overlapped with the next chapter's authoring to keep the pipeline moving. **Source-verified audit of the 5 questions flagged during the build** (3 parallel sub-agents, one per source paper, each rendering the scanned PDF + reading pages to locate/transcribe/verdict — see [[gdrive-pdf-fetch]]): **2 genuine wrong-key flips** — `ec2987e3` (Conics 2023-N2 Q51) B→A (parabola from `dy/dx=2y/x` through (1,2) is `x²=½y`, opens up → directrix `y=−1/8`) + `9e5b991b` (Trig-Eqn 2018-N2 Q40) B→A (printed `3(3−tan²A−cot A)²=1` is satisfied by 300° giving 1, not 315° giving 27 — stem NOT garbled, the stored solution had silently assumed a different equation); **2 stem-extraction fixes** (keys already right) — `bbddd3e0` (Inverse Trig Q26) `cos⁻¹(5/3)`→`cos⁻¹(√5/3)` [5/3 undefined] + options C/D `√3±√5`→`3±√5`, and `88fd7bc8` (Conics Q55) ellipse sign `−`→`+` (stored a hyperbola form); **1 false alarm** — `703e9ade` (Conics Q81) key B confirmed, only the rambling solution cleaned. None of the 3 papers print an answer key (question-paper-only scans) → verdicts are re-derivation from the printed stem. content_hash recomputed via the real helper for the 4 text/key changes (a tsx one-off, then deleted); each question verified to have exactly one correct option. Reconfirms [[audit-probe-symmetry]] (2 more stealth wrong-keys on "audit-closed" NDA Maths, surfaced by notes-build re-derivation + source) + [[scope-verification-material-first]] (local PDFs resolved all 5).

- **2026-06-09 — NDA Maths Quadratic Equations /notes chapter (20th NDA Maths, 24th notes) + /browse card + dashboard-filter + quiz-count-up fixes.** A mixed session: more mobile/UX polish off live screenshots, then a full notes chapter. **/browse card** ([QuestionCard.tsx](src/app/browse/QuestionCard.tsx)): difficulty stopped wrapping to a 2nd line (`truncate` needs `min-w-0` on a flex item — the Add-button width was NOT the cause, an assumption the user disproved), then the header was restructured from one `items-start` row to **two stacked rows** (meta row + full-width question) to kill the dead right-gutter below the controls ([[mobile-render-gotchas]]). **Dashboard quiz** ([QuizBrowser.tsx](src/app/dashboard/quizzes/QuizBrowser.tsx)): filter dropdowns now **cascade** (each reflects the OTHER active filters via `optsGiven`, + a new selection clears any filter it makes impossible) so Subject=Maths no longer lists Biology's chapters; `assembleNextQuiz` now takes the real chapter name from `NOTES_CHAPTERS` (keeps the "&" — "matrices-determinants" → "Matrices & Determinants", titleCase fallback) + the 11 existing matrices quizzes backfilled; expanded-quiz stems/options wrapped in `min-w-0 overflow-x-auto` + `overflow-x-hidden` on the dashboard main. **Same 3 fixes applied to nda-tracker** (`index.jsx` cascade, `QuizResults.jsx` containment; the matrices chapter-name backfill was a user-run SQL since the MCP is PYQ-Vault-bound). **Quiz score count-up** (both apps): the rAF-driven number could stick at 0 on mobile while the CSS ring finished — added a `setTimeout(ms+120)` settle that guarantees the final value regardless of rAF (the code was byte-identical to the working PYQ-Vault copy, so it's a defensive-correctness fix, not a logic bug). **Quadratic Equations notes**: 63 q · 3 subtopics · 21 concepts · 100% coverage · 2 diagrams (`qe-discriminant-parabola`, `qe-roots-in-interval`); REORDER-ONLY (guide refs the Vieta subtopic + ω+Vieta compound). **THE PROCESS MISS:** I first proposed *Definite Integration* and ran a full Step-0 on it before discovering it was **already shipped** (commit `362215b`, in `chapters.ts`) — because I checked which chapters are noted against the **stale CLAUDE.md header prose** instead of the `chapters.ts` registry / `npm run stats`. The header lagged 4 chapters (Sets & Relations, Definite Integration, Differential Equations were all done). Header line fixed + a guard added; lesson → [[project-docs-staleness]] (new "noted-chapters list" state class). Also strengthened the chapter's foundation after the user asked "does it cover foundation?" — I'd built the concept list bottom-up from HARD+MODERATE solutions, which *assume* the prerequisites; lesson → [[notes-structure-pedagogy-first]] (the teach-from-zero foundation pass). Cleaned one muddled Vieta "sum of all roots" solution (key B=12 correct — sum of all 4 roots incl. complex, not just the 2 real). notes:lint 0/0, notes:latex OK, prepush PASS.

- **2026-06-09 — Quiz UI redesign (both apps) + mobile-responsive polish pass.** Reworked the quiz-taking experience into a designed journey and fixed a batch of mobile-layout defects the user surfaced from live screenshots. **Quiz redesign (public `/quiz/[slug]` `QuizTaker.tsx` + nda-tracker `quizTaking.jsx`, agreed "both apps" + "one-at-a-time" via AskUserQuestion):** one-question-at-a-time with a progress bar, big circular A/B/C/D option chips, tap → check + auto-advance (~280ms) + keyboard (1-4 / arrows), a review-grid step, a slide-up bottom-sheet gate (public funnel only; key still server-side), and an animated results screen — SVG score ring + `requestAnimationFrame` count-up + a verdict headline + colored stat pills + Share + targeted review with notes deep-links. New pure `src/lib/quiz/verdict.ts` (`scoreVerdict` score-bands, TDD). nda-tracker has no `tailwindcss-animate`, so its animations are plain CSS transitions (mount-flip + stroke-dashoffset ring); public funnel review defaults to **misses-only**, the nda-tracker student review defaults to **all** (learning tool). **Mobile fixes (all CSS/layout, see [[mobile-render-gotchas]]):** (1) wide KaTeX math now wraps in `min-w-0 overflow-x-auto` per cell (was overflowing the viewport on matrices quizzes) + `overflow-x-hidden` on the quiz `<main>`; (2) `PrimaryNav` is icon-only below `sm:` (the 3 tabs + exam pill + account didn't fit at 360px); (3) `/browse` Add-to-paper button icon-only on phones; (4) `/browse` card meta is one non-wrapping line (breadcrumb `min-w-0 truncate`, difficulty `shrink-0` — `truncate` needs `min-w-0` to engage on a flex item); (5) `/browse` card header restructured from one `items-start` row to **two stacked rows** (meta row + full-width question) to kill the dead right-gutter below the controls. **Process lesson banked ([[diagnose-from-data-not-assumptions]]):** I assumed the cause twice (Add-button width for the difficulty wrap; trailing newlines for the whitespace) and was wrong both times — the user reset me to "always ground analysis on the data"; a `SELECT` of the raw row + reading the exact CSS gave the right cause. **Deferred (SUGGESTIONS):** underlined English/Biology words are stored as `\(\underline{\text{…}}\)` (a KaTeX math zone) so they render in KaTeX's font mid-prose + break line-clamp — bypass KaTeX for that pattern in the web renderer (mirrors the docx `UNDERLINE_BYPASS_RE`), not yet done.

- **2026-06-09 — Quiz Factory hardening + 5 chapters complete: immutable snapshots (0035), CLI-only assembly, `quiz:delete` propagation, bundle-formula split, ~580 hand-authored MCQs.** A long session turning the Quiz Factory from "2 chapters + lopsided formulas" into a robust, 5-chapter pipeline. Full state in [[daily-quiz-pilot]]; runbook [QUIZ_FACTORY.md](QUIZ_FACTORY.md). **The shipped pieces:**
  - **Bundle-formula SPLIT (replaced the lead-formula compromise).** A concept whose `formula.latex` bundles several identities (`\qquad`/`\quad`-joined) can't be one fair "which is THE formula?" MCQ. `harvestFormulaConcept` now SPLITS it into one `needs_review` slot per piece (`splitFormulaPieces`); each genuine formula is hand-authored with a SPECIFIC stem + **tempting permutation distractors** (wrong versions of the same formula, same option-shape — no length/format tell). Use judgment per slot: skip conditions/connectives/annotations/trivial-setups/off-target-prereqs/cross-concept-duplicates (parked, harmless). 80 formula-recall MCQs across statistics/probability/matrices/vectors.
  - **Immutable quiz snapshots (migration 0035).** `quizzes` gained `questions jsonb` + `theme` — `assembleNextQuiz` snapshots the assembled questions onto the row at assemble time; `listAssembledQuizzes` reads the snapshot (legacy pre-0035 quizzes fall back to the live `quiz_atoms_map` join). **Why:** the dashboard re-derived questions from LIVE atoms, so re-harvesting/re-classifying an atom already in a quiz rendered it broken (the optionless-bundle bug). A snapshot decouples display from the pool → PYQ Vault + nda-tracker can't drift, and a later atom change can't break a recorded quiz. `quiz_atoms_map` stays only as the coverage ledger. **The durable corollary: fixing an atom that's already in a quiz requires RE-ASSEMBLING that quiz** (the snapshot is frozen); `quiz:lint`'s new "quiz integrity" section lists quizzes mapping changed/non-ready atoms.
  - **Assembly is CLI-only now.** Removed the `/dashboard/quizzes` Assemble control + `assembleQuizAction` (UI was friction; user prefers CLI). Dashboard is a read-only view (pool stats + quiz list + Publish-to-public). `npm run quiz:assemble` is the path.
  - **`quiz:delete <slug>` with cross-app propagation.** `src/lib/quiz/deleteQuiz.ts` deletes the quiz here (cascades the map, frees atoms) AND POSTs `{action:"delete", id}` to nda-tracker's import endpoint (which now handles delete, **guarded to `status='draft'`** so a published quiz with attempts is never removed). Lets a stale/superseded quiz be cleaned from both apps in one command.
  - **Clean reset + 5 chapters complete.** Deleted all 24 pre-split draft quizzes (both apps) and re-assembled a fresh snapshot-backed set. Then **finished the 3 partial chapters** by hand-authoring distractors for their `needs_review` backlogs (the harvest's auto-candidates are cross-category/absurd, so all distractors are hand-written): **Human Physiology** (87 recall + 23 traps), **Matrices & Determinants** (182 computation + 5 traps), **Vectors** (130 computation + 55 traps) = **~480 MCQs** on top of the 80 formula ones. Statistics + Probability + HP + Matrices + Vectors are now complete; the ~15 unharvested chapters are the remaining frontier.
  - **Two correctness fixes** off user spot-checks: (1) the HP vitamins reference table had Vitamin K's chemical name as `—`, so the harvest made a broken "K → —" MCQ + leaked `—` as a distractor — filled in **Phylloquinone** at the source, re-harvested, 0 dash-options bank-wide. (2) `assembleNextQuiz` numbered the next slug as `count+1`, which COLLIDES when a middle quiz was deleted (a gap) and silently overwrites — fixed to **`max(suffix)+1`** + a regression test. Lesson: any auto-incrementing sequence that allows mid-deletes must use max+1, not count+1.

- **2026-06-09 — Public lead-magnet quiz funnel (Phase 1 + 2): cold traffic takes a public quiz → captured as a mobile lead → converts in-app.** Goal: share quiz links on forums/WhatsApp to generate leads — a funnel that belongs HERE not nda-tracker (this app is public, Next.js gives native OG/SEO, and it already assembles quizzes from /notes). Full state + deferred items in [[project-public-quiz-funnel]]. **Decisions:** (1) **Identity is MOBILE, not email** — PYQ Vault accounts are email-keyed and store no phone, so a lead can't be auto-matched to an account; fine, the funnel targets NEW prospects and the mobile feeds LWS sales. (2) **Answer key never reaches the client pre-submit** (the security spine): the public page fetches via the service-role admin client, STRIPS `answer`/`correct`, renders only stem+options; grading is server-side on submit (key revealed only in the POST response, after capture). No anon RLS on `quiz_atoms`. (3) **Gate at the reward** — anyone takes the quiz frictionlessly; name+mobile+DPDP-consent is asked right before the score. (4) **"All published quizzes are public"** — gate = the new `quizzes.public_slug` (NULL=private, set=public + the shareable URL), an explicit "Publish to public" admin action decoupled from the push lifecycle. (5) **Retake-aware** — `UNIQUE(quiz_id, mobile)` + the `record_quiz_lead` RPC bumps `attempts`/`best_score` (one lead per person-per-quiz; retakes are a quality signal); leads dashboard rolls up by mobile. (6) **Premium CTA gated on `billingLive`** (`isBillingConfigured()`) — free path today, premium CTA auto-appears once Razorpay env is set. (7) **Attribution-only** lead→buyer link (`utm_source=quiz:<slug>` rides signup → `user_metadata.signup_source`; phone-link deferred). **Shipped:** migrations **0033** (`public_slug` + `quiz_leads` + RLS) + **0034** (`record_quiz_lead` upsert RPC); pure `grade.ts`/`leads.ts`/`stemLint.ts`/`submit.ts` + server `publicQuiz.ts`; `POST /api/public-quiz/submit` (rate-limited, zod, consent-gated); public `/quiz/[slug]` (ISR, generateMetadata, per-quiz `opengraph-image.tsx`) + `QuizTaker`; "Publish to public" + Copy-link on `/dashboard/quizzes`; `/dashboard/leads` (rollup, CSV, wa.me); `/privacy`; `/notes` "Test yourself" CTA via `resolvePublicQuizForChapter`; `getClientIp`→`src/lib/http.ts`. TDD across grade/leads/stemLint/publicQuiz/submit. **Open:** nothing public until an admin clicks Publish; premium dormant until Razorpay live ([[project-paywall-build]]).

- **2026-06-09 — Quiz quality tooling: `quiz:lint` stem self-containment + `leadFormula` distractor declutter + name-based formula stems.** Surfaced by a real unfair public-quiz question. Root cause: the harvester lifts notes practiceSet/selfCheck prompts (authored as a CONNECTED sequence — item 2 says "the product" because item 1 defined it) into standalone, shuffled atoms, so back-references lose their antecedent; and formula stems inherited pedagogical `formula.label` nicknames ("Sieve Inequality") meaningless standalone. Fixes (all TDD; runbook [QUIZ_FACTORY.md](QUIZ_FACTORY.md)): **(a)** `src/lib/quiz/stemLint.ts` `flagStem` + `scripts/quiz/lint.ts` / **`npm run quiz:lint [route] [chapter]`** (triage, not a gate) — cut Statistics review from 185→1. **(b)** `harvestFormulaConcept` uses `concept.name` not `formula.label` (re-keys fingerprint). **(c)** `leadFormula()` trims sibling-formula distractors to their leading expression (splits on `\qquad`/`\quad` only, never commas; strips trailing comma); correct answer keeps full form. Regenerated all 4 formula chapters + synced — diff was stems+fingerprints+options only, **0 `correct`/`answer` changes**. **Durable lesson** ([[generator-output-regeneration]]): committed generator-output files (the atoms JSON) diff on regeneration after a *generator-code* change — not content drift; regenerate ALL affected + verify invariants before commit.

- **2026-06-09 — vitest `retry: 1` to absorb shared-DB integration flake.** The integration suite runs against ONE live Supabase project with vitest running files in parallel, so a test intermittently fails from cross-file data interference or a transient pooler/network blip (global-teardown already notes the "races with other files mid-flight"). `retry:1` lets the gate self-heal; a real failure still fails both attempts, so regressions aren't masked. See [[shared-db-test-flake]] (two pushes this session were rejected by a 1-of-1260 flake that passed clean on re-run, before this landed).

- **2026-06-09 — NDA Statistics daily quizzes complete (all themes).** 139 atoms verified + 17 trap MCQs authored → **12 quizzes** (1 Formulas, 1 Properties, 8 Practice, 1 Common Traps, + 1 prior) pushed to nda-tracker. State in [[daily-quiz-pilot]].

- **2026-06-08 (latest) — Daily Quiz Factory: cross-app pipeline turning /notes into themed daily recall quizzes delivered via nda-tracker.** New subsystem — **operational runbook: [QUIZ_FACTORY.md](QUIZ_FACTORY.md)** (the living how-to + per-chapter status; keep it updated). Full state + cadence in [[daily-quiz-pilot]]; cross-app lessons in [[cross-app-feature-execution]]. **Goal:** a daily 15-question Level-1 recall quiz (formulas/properties/traps — NOT PYQ deep-practice; that's a different job), delivered through **nda-tracker's existing Daily Quiz** feature (separate app + separate Supabase `exjnzrrlzcrsoxfoojcq`). **Architecture — two apps, one spine:** PYQ Vault is the content **Factory** (owns the question pool + classification); nda-tracker is **delivery** (batch/publish/attempts/scores). Metadata (per-question `conceptSlug`, quiz-level `exam`/`chapter`/`theme`) ORIGINATES in PYQ Vault and **rides on the push** — nda-tracker stores a copy for its own filtering. Never pull; never duplicate delivery state into PYQ Vault. **Schema (3 migrations):** `0030 quiz_atoms` (the harvested-MCQ pool — admin-read / service-role-write, no org_id like taxonomy; `status` auto|needs_review|verified; `source_fingerprint`=sha1(source notes) drives staleness; `atom_key`=natural upsert key; `trap_hints[]`), `0031 quizzes` (`id`=pushed UUID=`slugToUuid(slug)`, status draft→pushed→published) + `quiz_atoms_map` (coverage = a JOIN, not a lint), `0032 quiz_atoms.theme` (formula|property|computation|fact|trap, backfilled from source_kind). **Pipeline (`scripts/quiz/` + `src/lib/quiz/`):** `quiz:harvest` (ConceptUnit → atoms: formula/reference → **auto** key+distractors correct by construction; practiceSet/selfCheck → **needs_review** key-correct + rough candidates; trap → "spot the mistake" seed; trap_hints carried on every atom for the verify pass) → `quiz:sync` (JSON→DB, staleness-preserving: skips verified-unchanged, re-writes changed) → `quiz:verify` (promote needs_review→verified; **Claude hand-authors the 3 distractors per chapter** into `scripts/quiz/verify/<name>.ts` — user declined both a UI form and a bulk pass, cadence is chapter-by-chapter on request) → `quiz:assemble` (ready atoms → quiz; **theme-filterable**, variety-interleaved when mixed, coverage-dedup vs `quiz_atoms_map`, records `quizzes`+map, pushes draft) → push. **Idempotency:** deterministic slug→UUIDv5, so re-push UPDATEs not duplicates. **Themed quizzes (user's call):** a daily recall drill wants ONE objective/day (Formulas day, Traps day…) — mixed-like-the-exam is the *mock test's* job; rotate themes across the week. **UI:** `/dashboard/quizzes` (admin) — pool StatCards + an Assemble control (chapter + theme dropdowns → server action `assembleQuizAction`, service-role, env-gated push) + a **filterable** assembled-quiz list (`QuizBrowser`, client; exam/subject/chapter/theme/status; **theme derived from the quiz's actual atom content** — single distinct theme → that, else "mixed" — same rule the assembler uses, so the title/pushed-theme/dashboard all agree). nda-tracker got the mirror filter bar on its Daily Quiz page (commit `e142ca0`). **Live state:** Quiz Factory in prod, 1018 atoms across 5 chapters, ~11 quizzes built (mostly pushed); full loop (assemble in browser → push → publish → student takes → results) validated. **Deferred:** approve more chapters (refills ready pool), QuizEditor classification fields in nda-tracker (so hand-made quizzes are filterable), student-portal filters. (The quiz core was relocated `scripts/quiz/` → `src/lib/quiz/` same-day, killing the `src→scripts` import smell; CLIs are thin runners importing the core.)

Replaced with registry-derived hubs: **`lib/notes/notesNav.ts`** (pure `getNotesExamGroups` / `getNotesExamGroup`, grouping `NOTES_CHAPTERS` by exam → subject; TDD `tests/notes-nav.test.ts`, 6 cases) feeds a new **dynamic `/notes/[examSlug]`** per-exam hub (lists that exam's notes subjects; honest "coming soon" empty state for JEE; coexists with the concrete `nda-maths`/`nda-biology`/… subject folders — concrete route wins) and a rewritten **`/notes` cross-exam index** (was a `redirect("/notes/nda-maths")`). `examContext.notesPath` retargeted to `/notes/<slug>` for all three exams (NDA → `/notes/nda`, MHT-CET → `/notes/mht-cet`, JEE → `/notes/jee-mains`); Footer collapsed from 4 per-subject links → 2 per-exam hub links; sitemap adds the `/notes` index + each hub. **Net: adding a future exam/subject's notes needs only a `NOTES_CHAPTERS` entry (+ the subject's `page.tsx`) — nav/footer/sitemap all auto-derive.** `tests/exam-context.test.ts` updated to the new hub hrefs. Gate: typecheck, notes:lint 0/0, full prepush PASS. The earlier same-session `fix` (added `/notes/nda-biology/page.tsx` + a per-subject footer link) is what surfaced this — the per-subject footer link it added was then superseded by the per-exam hub. Lesson → [[notes-self-sufficient-template]] (a new SUBJECT still needs its `page.tsx`; the footer no longer does).

- **2026-06-07 — Word-export fix: math accents (\bar, \hat, \vec, \dot) + \overline now render properly.** User screenshot: the mean bar in an exported `.docx` showed as a tiny detached mark floating above-right of `x`, not a bar. Root cause (verified by dumping the OMML): `mml2omml` maps temml's single-base accents (`\bar`/`\hat`/`\vec`/`\dot`/`\tilde`, all `<mover>` in MathML) to an over-LIMIT **`<m:limUpp>`** — Word draws a limit at reduced size, detached above the base. And `\overline` maps to a top-only **`<m:borderBox>`** which renders invisibly under our injected math defaults (the same failure as the documented `\underline` borderBox). Both are now repaired by a new post-process **`wrapAccents`** in [ommlBuilder.ts](src/lib/export/ommlBuilder.ts), chained after `wrapMatrixDelimiters` (same render-only post-process pattern as the matrix-delimiter + underline fixes — **no DB/bank writes**; existing papers regenerate correctly on next download): a `<m:limUpp>` whose `<m:lim>` is exactly one recognised accent char → `<m:acc>` with the matching COMBINING char (overline U+0305, circumflex U+0302, arrow U+20D7, dot U+0307, tilde U+0303); a top-only `<m:borderBox>` (i.e. `\overline`) → `<m:bar pos=top>`. (A bottom-only borderBox — `\underline` in math — is deliberately LEFT as-is: the `\underline` text bypass + the preserved borderBox fallback are a separate intentional design — see `tests/underline-roundtrip.test.ts`.) A `<m:limUpp>` with any other lim content (real `\lim`/`\overset`/`\overbrace`) and `\overrightarrow` (already a correct stretchy `<m:groupChr>`) are left untouched. Scope was broad — PUBLIC field-occurrences: `\bar` 127, `\hat` 485, `\vec` 625, `\overline` 46, `\dot` 4 (`\overrightarrow` 125 already fine). +8 TDD cases in `tests/omml-builder.test.ts` (38 total in that file). Browser/KaTeX rendering was never affected — export-only.

- **2026-06-06 — Bank-wide control-char LaTeX repair + /notes run-on-definition bulleting.** Two readability fixes off user review of the new Functions notes. **(1) Bank corruption (DB, no code):** a rendered card showed `KaTeX parse error: Expected '\right'` — root cause was an ingestion step that processed string escapes, turning LaTeX commands whose backslash is followed by `r`/`t` into **control characters**: `\right`/`\rho`/`\rightarrow` → `CR`, `\text`/`\times`/`\tan`/`\textit` → `TAB`. A bank-wide scan (raw SQL — NOT supabase-js, which silently 1000-row-caps; the dry-run that missed it is the textbook PostgREST 1000-row-cap trap — see "Recurring pitfalls") found the true scope is **small**: 6 CR-command + 26 TAB-command field/option occurrences; backspace/formfeed 0 (so `\frac` was safe), and the 256 CR rows that are just benign CRLF line-endings + all LF-before-letter cases (real `\n\n` newlines / GFM tables) were correctly left alone. Fixed with `regexp_replace(col, chr(13)||'([a-zA-Z])', '\\r\1')` + the `chr(9)→\t` analogue across text/context/solution + options. **content_hash recomputed for the 5 rows whose text/option changed** (those fields are in the hash via `norm` which collapses whitespace; context/solution are not) using the real `contentHash` helper by explicit-id fetch. Verified 0 remaining. **(2) /notes run-on definitions (TS):** 9 `definition` fields that packed 4–7 inline-math identities into one semicolon-joined prose line (the "wall" the user flagged) → converted to `- ` bulleted lists with bold labels (RichText renders `<ul>`, per the existing convention for property enumerations). Concepts: Matrices `det-products-scalar-powers` + `inverse-properties` + `transpose-rules`; Vectors `cross-product-algebra-and-properties` + `perpendicularity-test`; Probability `min-max-of-combined-probabilities` + `probability-identity-statements`; Matrices `adjoint-properties`; MHT-CET `identity-simplification`. A scan confirmed the bold-led / numbered-procedure definitions (3D Geometry etc.) were already fine and left untouched. notes:latex + notes:lint 0/0; markup-only (TDD-exempt per [[tdd-calibration]]).

- **2026-06-06 — /browse subtopics + chapter-scoped questions sort by TEACHING ORDER (migration 0029), for the ~11 noted chapters only.** User flagged that the /browse filter sidebar listed a chapter's subtopics by question-count desc (Matrices & Determinants showed "Determinant Properties (59)" first), not in the order the chapter is taught. Root cause: `mergeAndSortFacets` ([src/lib/questions/facets.ts](src/lib/questions/facets.ts)) sorted purely by count, and **subtopics had no order column** (chapters have `order_index`; subtopics never did). Teaching order existed only in each /notes chapter's `subtopicOrder`, which /browse never read. **Shipped, scoped to "notes-only, rest unchanged"** (user's explicit call):
  - **Migration 0029** adds `subtopics.order_index int` — **NULLABLE, default NULL** (deliberately NOT `0` like chapters): NULL = "no teaching order" and sorts LAST in both the JS facet sort (`?? Infinity`) and the SQL question sort (`nullsFirst:false`). A `0` default would sort an un-ordered subtopic ahead of a position-1 one. So the ~168 chapters outside notes keep byte-identical count-desc behaviour.
  - **`scripts/sync-subtopic-order.ts`** (`npm run notes:order`, idempotent, `--dry`) derives order_index from `NOTES_CHAPTERS` → each chapter's `subtopicOrder` (position → 1-based index), joining notes `subtopicName` → DB subtopic by name. LOUD on the name-join caveat ([[shipped-chapter-rename-downstream-sync]]): reports any notes slug with no live DB subtopic AND any DB subtopic left unordered. First run: 11 chapters, 55 subtopics ordered, 0 issues. Added as the post-ship step in the Notes editorial workflow (also re-syncs after a Phase-D re-carve).
  - **Sidebar:** `mergeAndSortFacets` sort precedence is now `orderIndex asc (null last) → count desc → name`. Subtopics pass `orderIndex`; **chapters pass nothing** → all tie on the order term → identical to before (chapter ordering deliberately untouched, honouring "rest as-is"). TDD: 4 new cases in `tests/facets.test.ts`.
  - **Questions:** when a chapter is filtered, `query.ts` leads the sort with the subtopic's teaching order, then the existing `created_at desc → source_row → id`. The unfiltered /browse default (newest-first) is untouched (a global subtopic-order sort would meaninglessly interleave unrelated chapters). DB-integration assert in `tests/query-order.test.ts` (order_index `[1,2,null]`, proving NULLs-last).
  - **PostgREST gotcha (cost ~30 min, worth banking):** ordering the PARENT result by an embedded to-one column does NOT work via supabase-js's `.order(col, { referencedTable })` — that orders the embedded child *collection*, a no-op for a to-one embed (probe returned unsorted). The working form is to pass the **embed-alias column string** `.order("subtopic(order_index)", { ascending, nullsFirst:false })` with NO `referencedTable` — supabase-js forwards it as PostgREST's top-level `order=subtopic(order_index)` spec (uses the SELECT *alias* `subtopic`, not the table name `subtopics`). Verified live: `1×33 2×22 3×59 4×20 5×28 6×8` = exact Matrices teaching order. See [[postgrest-order-by-embedded-column]].

- **2026-06-04 — /notes mastery-check + /guide worked-example cards now render GFM pipe-tables.** Bug (user-reported, screenshot): the `SubtopicMasteryCheckpoint` + `/guide` worked-example cards rendered bank passage/stem content through plain `KatexRenderer`, so a GFM pipe-table in a question's `context`/`text` (the Probability Q112 contingency table, surfaced on a CET notes mastery card) showed as raw `| … |` markup. Fix: `WorkedExampleCard` ([src/app/guide/_components/WorkedExampleCard.tsx](src/app/guide/_components/WorkedExampleCard.tsx)) swaps `KatexRenderer` → the table-aware `BlockText` for `example.context` + `example.text` ONLY — mirroring `/browse`; options + solution stay on `KatexRenderer` (the v1 no-tables-in-options contract). `BlockText` fast-paths non-table text to byte-identical `KatexRenderer` output, so the 13 linearized Statistics questions are unaffected. Pure render-wiring (table parsing already TDD'd in `parseTableBlocks`, so no new test per [[tdd-calibration]]); typecheck + full prepush green, merged to `main` (`028836a`). **Surface lesson:** bank `text`/`context` now flows through `BlockText` on FOUR surfaces — /browse expanded card, editor Preview, docx export, and the /notes+/guide `WorkedExampleCard`; a future content-render capability on those fields must cover all four. Fixed by swapping `subtopicOrder` alone (no slug/name change → zero Phase-D / guide-sync cost). **(2) Coverage: +6 concepts, all bank-validated** — Foundations `linear-argument-rule`; Substitution `reciprocal-power-substitution` (split off the `u-substitution-basics` catch-all to hold the signature HARD take-out-power / `t=x±k/x` family, 7 q incl. the 6× near-dup `x²(x⁴+1)^{3/4}`); Trig I `standard-trig-integrals` (∫tan/cot/sec/cosec, foundation) + `inverse-trig-argument`; Trig II `product-of-sines` + `trig-to-partial-fraction` (carved out of an overloaded `weierstrass` tag that was absorbing 3 distinct techniques). Enhancements: `divide-by-cos²` gained the `tan x = t` double-angle route; `standard-quadratic-forms` gained the `sec⁻¹` + `∫f'/√f` forms. **DB: ~13 `question_concept_tags` re-tagged — `concept_slug` only, `subtopic_slug` unchanged** — so the rework is migration-cheap (no PK invalidation, no guide/browse cross-ref breakage); distribution rebalanced (`u-sub-basics` 20→16, `weierstrass` 7→2; every bank-anchored concept keeps ≥1 tag). **Lesson:** a `reference`-variant concept must be bank-tagged — a no-PYQ teaching primitive (the four ∫trig results have no pure PYQ) must be a `formula`-variant FOUNDATION (no `pyqExampleId` → lint-exempt), NOT a reference table (notes-lint warns: reference = the flat-fact-recall bucket, never a primitive). Gates: notes:lint 0/0, notes:latex OK, prepush green (1091 tests). Ordering + overloaded-tag + shipped-chapter-review lessons → [[notes-structure-pedagogy-first]]; reference-must-be-tagged reconfirmed → [[notes-self-sufficient-template]].

- **2026-06-04 — Solution cleanup: NDA Maths, 28 "closed" chapters swept for stealth wrong-keys — 21 key flips.** Ran /solution-cleanup across every NDA Maths chapter except Binomial Distribution + Indefinite Integration (just done).   - **Conics** Q85/2022 A→B (`2c=√2`).

  - **Inverse Trig** Q37/2022 B→C (`x=1/√2`).
  - **Lines** Q60/2025 D→A (sum of intercepts `=2`); Q52/2020 D→C (`2x−3y=5`); Q56/2017 A→B (ratio `3:4`); Q87/2022 B→A (sum of \|intercepts\| `=2`; PDF-confirmed).
  - **Trig Equations** Q43/2020 A→B (`cot(α+β)=1/3`).
  - **Vectors** Q70/2017 A→B (`λ=3`).
  - **Height & Distance** Q100/2019 B→A (`z²=h²(cot²y−cot²x)` = option A; right angle at A ⇒ `OB²=OA²+AB²`).
  - **Quadratic Equations** Q4/2017 C→A (strict `<√5` excludes `k=±3` → open interval; PDF-confirmed stem).
  - **Definite Integration** set S7/2019: Q94 B→C (`I₁=π`) + Q95 D→A (`I₁+I₂=2π`) — both keys in the 2-item set were wrong; PDF-confirmed.
  **PDF-driven storage fixes (key unchanged):** Quad Q3/2025 stem `2/√3`→`2√3` (paper-confirmed; key B=6 holds); Binary Q6/2025 stem dropped a spurious trailing digit (`12345678987654321=111111111²`) + options were plain decimal not `(·)₂` (key D holds). **Printed-paper preserves (key + honest note):** Def-Int Q81/2023 — computed `(π+2)/(π−2)` is not among the options (sibling Q82 `8I₁²=π⁴` confirms `I₁`), official key B preserved; App-Deriv Q77/2022 — printed `m+8n` is indeterminate (=option D) while official key B=0 = the intended `m−8n`, preserved + note. ~19 further AGREE/keep-key de-hedge rewrites. **No PUBLIC-count change** (flips only). All Phase-5 probes return 0; all 22 hash-bearing rows recompute. **Lesson:** "closed" means closed *for the probes that ran*. BOTH gaps mattered here: the `REVIEW:`-marker sweep + fresh re-derivation caught 20, and once the disagree-probe was made **case-insensitive** it caught a 21st that had no hedge marker at all. The `/solution-cleanup` skill's disagree-probe was hardened to case-insensitive (`.claude/commands/solution-cleanup.md`, this session) so future runs catch lowercase `matches option` on the first pass. - **2026-06-04 — Custom domain + rebrand: "Question Bank" → "PYQ Vault" at https://www.pyqvault.com.** Bought `pyqvault.com` on GoDaddy (the available exact-keyword `.com` after scanning ~30 — "PYQ" matches what aspirants search; pyqforge/examvault/etc. all taken), pointed it at the existing Vercel `question-bank` project on **Hobby** (custom domains + free SSL work on Hobby; commercial Razorpay use will need **Pro** later). DNS at GoDaddy = single `A @ → 216.198.79.1` + `CNAME www → <unique>.vercel-dns-NNN.com` (delete GoDaddy's two parking `A @` records + edit the default `www` CNAME — "conflicts with another record" = editing the existing row, not adding; DNS is per-domain so other domains are untouched). www set primary, apex 308→www. Then the app-side follow-ups: **(1)** added the new domain to **Supabase Auth** redirect URLs + Site URL (mandatory or sign-in breaks); **(2)** swapped 11 hardcoded `question-bank-sage.vercel.app` refs → `https://www.pyqvault.com` (4 `SITE_URL` consts drive canonical/metadataBase/sitemap/robots/JSON-LD; 7 OG-image footer labels) — a redirect alone is NOT enough because canonical/OG/sitemap are *declared in the HTML* and read by crawlers, not redirected; **(3) rebrand** "Question Bank" → "PYQ Vault" across 17 files (`SITE_NAME` cascades to title/template/applicationName/OG/twitter; + AppHeader wordmark, login/signup, Razorpay checkout `name`, JSON-LD publisher "LWS Pune — PYQ Vault", OG-image titles, default export filename; per-page titles trimmed to "Premium"/"Your account" so the root template adds the brand once not twice). Left lowercase "question bank" prose (the corpus, e.g. "2,160-question bank") unchanged. Description refreshed: "MHT-CET, NDA and JEE Main **live**; IPMAT, CUET, NEET coming soon". See [[project-domain-brand]].

- **2026-06-03 — JEE Mains Papers 5-10 (2021) batch-ingested + PUBLIC; JEE now 599 q PUBLIC (Papers 1-10, 600 committed).** Six papers in one session via the mature pipeline; ~126 AI-authored solutions bank-wide (grounded in read figures / re-derivation, unicode-free). Three pipeline-hardening changes (all TDD/gate-clean, committed): **(1) subject-by-number** — `lib.ts` now derives subject from the question number (1-30 Phy / 31-60 Chem / 61-90 Maths) instead of parsing PART headers, after Paper 6's Chemistry header failed to match and mislabelled Q31-60 Physics (`subjectForNumber` + test); **(2) option-supply** — `commit.ts` lets `optionOverrides` supply all four options for a needs_review row whose options didn't parse (Paper 7 Q77 statement-style options split by a display-math block); **(3) skip-unplannable** — `attach-images.ts` warns+skips a record whose image layout `planFor` can't resolve (Paper 5 Q41's 2-scheme + 4-option layout) instead of throwing, so the rest of the paper still attaches. Recurring per-paper work this batch: **garbled dropped-digit values** (the source's leading-char-drop pattern — e.g. "0 cm"→20 cm, ".01×10⁵"→1.01×10⁵, "to  A"→3 A) back-solved from options/answers; **match-list / IE tables that didn't extract** restored as GFM tables (P6 Q34, P8 Q34, P10 Q45); **multi-figure stems** composited into one labelled image (P5 Q36 aromatic structures, P5 Q41 two reaction schemes — same Pillow technique as P4 Q44); **all-"1." solution-doc numbering** (P8 Physics) → answers + solutions supplied positionally; **stem-located LaTeX artifacts** (split `\(…\)`, trailing `\`, mangled `\begin{matrix}` piecewise) fixed via `stemOverride`+`resync` (these escape `attach-solutions`' solution-only KaTeX guard but `validate-db` catches them). `validate-db`'s soft "incomplete?" flags on text-only LCR / ammonolysis questions are false positives (stem mentions a circuit/reaction but the options are self-contained). Per-paper effort ranged ~40 min (clean papers 9/10) to ~90 min (Paper 8's all-"1." remap + 27 authored). Full state + per-paper notes in [[jee-mains-ingestion]]; NEXT: Papers 11-26.

- **2026-06-03 — JEE Mains Papers 2-3 + scale-up pipeline hardening + exam-picker wiring; Papers 1-3 now 180 q PUBLIC.** Wired `jee-mains` into `EXAM_REGISTRY` (guides/notes null → fall back to indexes; `/browse` filter was already DB-driven) so JEE is a pickable exam. Then ran the first scale-up paper (`2021-p2`), which stress-tested the pipeline and forced real hardening (all committed, 40 tests): **(segmenter/parser)** anchor a number alone on its line; keep matrix `\\` row separators (the hard-break strip was eating them); skip `(a)-(ii)` match-codes when finding option markers; **`findDuplicateSolutionNumbers`** warns on a mis-numbered soln block (Paper 2's soln had two "1." → Map last-wins silently **wrong-keyed Q1** + left Q2 keyless — only re-derivation caught it); dropped `sec` from auto-upright (collides with "m/sec"). **(per-paper overrides)** `PaperData` gained `stemOverrides` + `answerOverrides`; `isCommittable` recovers needs_review/no_answer_key rows instead of dropping them. **(robustness)** attach-images plans by option-emptiness not status (match-list-as-image → stem figure); cleanup-latex gained a KaTeX-validated **de-glue** (`\inR`→`\in R`, validates the prefix macro in isolation so multi-macro zones resolve). **THE re-commit-after-cleanup HAZARD:** cleanup-latex mutates committed text+hash, so re-running `commit` inserts **duplicates** (the recomputed hash no longer matches) — fixed by deleting dups and adding **`scripts/jee/resync.ts`** (re-applies overrides to committed rows + recomputes hash via direct UPDATE, no re-commit). **Validation gap (caught by a human /browse review, not the validator):** "0 KaTeX-broken" ≠ "renders clean" — KaTeX only checks complete segments, so a leaked trailing `\` (~19 stems, both papers) and **split `\(…\)` delimiters across options** (each option opened the next one's math, Q62/Q69) passed it; `validate-db` now scans for both + markup leaks. Solutions: user chose **full parity** — Paper 2 is 60/60 (16 authored, organic/circuit ones grounded in the read images). Pace: one more paper (P3) end-to-end, then batch. Per-paper effort is ~45-60 min (classify + a few overrides + ~16 authored + quirk-fixing) — NOT a hands-off loop. **Paper 3 (2021-p3) then ran fully autonomously (user authorized no-approvals) and was clean — the hardening absorbed every common quirk (no answer-key corruption, no number-alone misses). It added the last recurring artifact auto-repair: `cleanup-latex` now rebalances split `\(…\)` delimiters per field (drop a trailing dangling `\(`; prepend `\(` when a field starts with a `\)`), fixing all 15 of P3's artifacts in ONE pass vs P2's manual overrides — this pattern recurs every paper. P3 reached 60/60 solutions; Q37 is structurally incomplete (its reaction figure didn't extract — passes every render/leak/artifact check yet is unanswerable), **set PRIVATE** (179 JEE PUBLIC) with a tracking note in `papers/2021-p3.json`. This drove a 5th `validate-db` scan — a soft **completeness check** (visual-ref stem + no image + no display-math) that flags exactly this class.** See [[jee-mains-ingestion]] + [[docx-pandoc-ingestion]].

- **2026-06-03 — JEE Mains ingestion: new exam, 2021 Paper 1 pilot (60 MCQ) live + a parameterized `scripts/jee/` pipeline.** First new exam since the MHT-CET/NDA era. User had DOCX papers (`C:\tmp\PYQPs\JEE_Mains\`) and asked "do it in chat, not Excel/UI?" — the real answer was **neither**: the hard 95% is *extraction* (OMML→LaTeX, image extract, classify into a new taxonomy), which is mechanism-independent, so a one-off **scripted extractor → the existing `commitStaged` pipeline** keeps every invariant (dedup / taxonomy auto-create / content_hash) instead of re-implementing them in chat. **pandoc** (`--extract-media -t markdown`) converts the papers' OMML equations to faithful LaTeX *and* extracts figures inline — the linchpin (a local tool, not an app dep). Shipped end-to-end across the session: seeded `JEE Mains` exam + 3 subjects (Physics/Chemistry/Maths); built the pipeline TDD-first (`scripts/jee/lib.ts` pure helpers, 34 tests); committed 60 MCQ PRIVATE with a per-paper `upload_jobs` row; attached 41 images + solutions; flipped PUBLIC. **v1 choices:** MCQ-only (Section B numericals skipped), with images, fresh JEE taxonomy (47 ch · 57 sub auto-created), pilot-first. **13 image-only source solutions** were **AI-authored** after *reading the extracted reaction-scheme images* (not guessed) — flagged as authored, not source-derived. Then **parameterized per-paper** (`config.ts` + `papers/<paperId>.json` + `<paperId>` args; `classification.ts` retired) for the scale-up. Durable lessons → [[docx-pandoc-ingestion]] (the reusable pattern) + full state/IDs in [[jee-mains-ingestion]]. Key gotchas: KaTeX validation only catches *math* — raw image markdown / `<!-- -->` / attribute leaks need a separate text scan; Section A/B split is **positional** (each part = 20 MCQ + 10 numerical), not option-count; attach steps must scope by `source_file` since question numbers (Q1-90) repeat across papers.

The per-shift `*_QuestionBank.xlsx` are NOT independent — they ARE the bank's ingestion source (identical garbled options + same key), so only the rendered exam paper is authoritative. **Critical finding:** this paper set is a **coaching reproduction** ("Gyanam Test Generator, Kota") riddled with printed typos — so the bank's extraction was largely *faithful* and most "errors" are in the source papers, splitting the deferred items two ways. **(A) Bank extraction errors → FIXED (5):** `7d3fa448` stem was an LLM hallucination ("toluene+KMnO₄") — real reaction is **phenol + CrO₃ → p-benzoquinone**, key D was right (stem fixed); The DOCX→PDF Word-COM render pipeline is the reusable technique for any future MHT-CET source check ([[mhtcet-source-docx-render]]).
- **2026-06-03 — Word-export fix: matrices/determinants now render with stretchy brackets (`<m:d>` delimiters).** Bug: delimited matrix environments (`pmatrix` `(`, `bmatrix` `[`, **`vmatrix` `|` determinants**, `Bmatrix` `{`, `Vmatrix` `‖`) rendered in the `.docx` with **tiny, detached, single-line-height brackets beside the grid** instead of enclosing it. Root cause (verified by dumping `document.xml` from a real export): `mml2omml` converts the matrix grid to a proper OMML `<m:m>` but emits the surrounding stretchy fence operators as plain `<m:r><m:t>(</m:t></m:r>` text runs flanking it — never an OMML delimiter object, so Word has nothing to stretch. Fix: one pure helper `wrapMatrixDelimiters(omml)` in [ommlBuilder.ts](src/lib/export/ommlBuilder.ts), called from `latexToOmml` after `sanitizeOmmlForXml`, that rewrites the `fence-run + <m:m> + fence-run` shape into `<m:d><m:dPr><m:begChr/><m:endChr/><m:ctrlPr/></m:dPr><m:e><m:m>…</m:m></m:e></m:d>` with `begChr`/`endChr` taken from the captured fence chars (so `vmatrix` → real determinant bars). Only a fence run **immediately adjacent** to `<m:m>` is consumed → ordinary `f(x)` math and fence-less `\begin{matrix}` are untouched (negative tests lock this). **Render-only — no DB writes, no bank edits**; existing papers/keys render correctly on the next download (export regenerates from live data). +7 TDD cases in [omml-builder.test.ts](tests/omml-builder.test.ts) (995 → 1002 tests). Mirrors the existing "mml2omml limitation → post-process the OMML string" pattern already used for underline-bypass + XML-escape.
- **2026-06-03 — LaTeX cleanup: NDA · Chemistry · whole subject (262 q; 69 formula-bearing across 10 chapters).** The standard latex-cleanup probes were already **0** — the bank's chemistry formulae were de-facto **unicode-subscript** (49 q, e.g. `H₂O`), which the probe regex tolerates (it checks superscripts, not subscripts). The real state was a 3-way mix: 49 unicode-subscript + 17 ASCII `->` arrows + 13 bare-digit (`FeCl2`) + a few already-LaTeX. User chose **full-LaTeX canonicalization**: every formula → `\(\text{El}_n\)` with `\to` arrows, `\cdot` for hydrates, `\rightleftharpoons` for equilibria, charges as `^{n+}`. Converted across stems + options + solutions; **23 rows had text/option changes (hash-refreshed, all 23 verified), 46 were solution-only** (no hash impact). **No key flips** — mechanical formatting only (no `is_correct` touched). All Phase-5 probes (standard unicode/balance + chem arrow/subscript/bare-digit) return **0**. **Canonical chem form going forward = LaTeX `\(\text{}_n\)` + `\to`** — unicode `→` can't be the target (it trips the latex-cleanup probe), and this unifies the previously-mixed conventions. DB edits already live; this logs them.

This chapter sat inside the 2026-05-27 "NDA bank-wide closed" audit — all three flips are **stealth wrong-keys** (the bank solution agreed with the wrong key, so the matches-option probe never fired; caught only by fresh derivation, two via a hand sweep the probes didn't surface). Key flips:
  - Q76 2022 NDA 2 · `f9259cbb`: A→C. \(\int\frac{(\cos x)^{1.5}-(\sin x)^{1.5}}{\sqrt{\sin x\cos x}}=2\sqrt{\sin x}+2\sqrt{\cos x}\); stored key A dropped the factor 2.
  - Q85 2021 NDA 1 · `4ce9c717`: B→D. \(\int\frac{dx}{\sec x+\tan x}=\ln|\sec x+\tan x|-\ln|\sec x|\); the stored solution swapped \(\int\sec x\) and \(\int\tan x\), landing on the negated key B.
  - Q81 2019 NDA 2 · `84d75bd9`: A→D. \(\int\frac{dx}{2x^2-2x+1}=\tan^{-1}(2x-1)\); stored key A \(=\tan^{-1}(2x-1)/2\) — an extra ½ from a \(\frac12\cdot2=\frac12\) arithmetic slip (half the true value).
  - Q100 2024 NDA 2 · `8afc550b`: D→A (part of the broken-stem set below).
  **Broken-stem set (S20, 2024 NDA 2, Q99+Q100) — PDF-confirmed** against `Maths_2024_NDA2.pdf` (Drive, Set A): printed integrand is \((x^2-1)/\sqrt{x^2+1}\) — the bank had **dropped the √** (stored \((x^2+1)\)), making the printed \(U\!\cdot\!V-3\ln(U+V)\) form impossible (\(2\int\frac{x^2-1}{x^2+1}=2x-4\tan^{-1}x\), no product/log). Fixed the shared context to \(\sqrt{x^2+1}\); with \(U=x,\,V=\sqrt{x^2+1}\): Q99 \(|U^2-V^2|=1\) (key B kept; restored the \(|\cdot|\) the bank dropped from the stem), Q100 \(U\!\cdot\!V=\sqrt{x^2+x^4}\) so **D→A** (the bank had ALSO dropped the √ from all four options — restored them; C reconstructed as \(\tfrac12\sqrt{x^2+x^4}\) from the scalar-distractor pattern, OCR garbled there). The set sibling rule applied (both items in S20 fixed together; context propagated to both).
  Prose-only rewrites (key already correct): Q91 2026 NDA 1 · `8501bf79` (A=1/5, had a "…wait…Let me redo" fragment); Q85 u+v 2025 NDA 2 · `9967b15a` (=dv/dx, had "eˣcosx·0…"/"Actually" noise). No cluster bias (wrong keys spread A/B/A/D). All Phase-5 probes return 0; all 5 content_hashes match recomputed. NDA Maths PUBLIC count unchanged at 2,160. (Lesson reinforced: [[audit-probe-symmetry]] — a "closed" chapter can still hide stealth wrong-keys a probe can't see; the HARD re-derivation sweep is what caught Q85 + Q81.)

- **2026-06-02 — LaTeX cleanup: MHT-CET · Physics · whole subject (24 chapters, 1,577 PUBLIC q).** 39 questions touched (Bucket A, mechanical): straggler unicode → LaTeX (`Ω`→`\(\Omega\)`, `μ`→`\(\mu\)`, `²³`→`\(^n\)`, `×`→`\(\times\)`, `→`→`\(\to\)`, `cm⁻¹`→`cm\(^{-1}\)`) across qtext/solution/options; hashes refreshed on the 13 rows whose text/options changed. No unbalanced delimiters or matrix envs anywhere — subject was already clean (2.8% flagged). 9 deferred to /solution-cleanup: 7 hedge/REVIEW/corrupted-stem solutions (incl. `7678e0af` garbled NAND truth-table, `1f10ec95` Rotational MI wrong-key candidate) + 2 clean-LaTeX wrong-key candidates outside the unicode set (`c4d0f63c` AC Q43 soln→A/key C, `0f518fb9` Rotational Q36 soln→B/key D — both 2024 May HARD). Phase 5 probes return 0 except the 7 deferred (1 qtext + 6 solution).
- **2026-06-02 — In-app /notes concept reporting + admin triage (mirrors question-reports).** Logged-in users report a concept from its card (incorrect-content / confusing-explanation / typo / broken-visualization / wrong-example / other); admins triage at /dashboard/notes-reports with an open-count badge. Built TDD-first; migration 0028 + lib/notes-reports + 2 API routes + ReportConceptDialog + triage page; +19 tests (980 total); gate + advisors green.
  - **The load-bearing decision — org resolution for org-less content.** The user chose "org-scoped like questions" over a global model, but the schema then forced the interesting part: the ENTIRE taxonomy (exams/subjects/chapters/subtopics) has **no `org_id`** — only questions do — and a concept is TS editorial with no DB row at all. So a concept genuinely has no owning org. Resolved by routing the report to **the org that owns the bank questions the concept teaches**: `createConceptReport` resolves the subtopic via `resolveTaxonomy`, takes the `org_id` of a PUBLIC question in it, and denormalizes it. Reliable because every notes subtopic has bank questions by construction (works for tag-less foundation concepts too), unambiguous today (one org owns all PUBLIC questions), and a clean parallel to the question-report model. A DB test asserts the report lands on the content org, NOT the reporter's throwaway org.
  - **ISR preserved by client-side auth.** `/notes` pages are ISR-cached (cookie-free anon client). The browse `ReportQuestionDialog` gets `isLoggedIn` from the server — doing that on notes would force dynamic rendering and kill the cache on every page. Instead `ReportConceptDialog` resolves login **client-side** (browser supabase `getUser` on mount); the build confirms free notes pages stayed SSG-prerendered (●) even though the server-component `ConceptUnitCard` now renders the client dialog. Anon → sign-in prompt + concept-prefilled mailto.
  - **Identity + reuse.** Concept identity is the globally-unique `(subtopic_slug, concept_slug)` pair (same key as `question_concept_tags`); `getConceptIdentity` validates it against `NOTES_CHAPTERS` so a tampered POST for a non-existent concept is rejected (404). The `report_status` enum + status validators are REUSED from 0024 (lifecycle is identical) — only the category enum is new.
  - Also fixed a pre-existing dead mailto: the question-report fallback pointed at `report@questionbank.lwspune.org` (doesn't exist) → `connect.lwspune@gmail.com` (matches the Footer). Both report dialogs now consistent.

- **2026-06-02 — Bank-wide /notes worked-example de-duplication + a lint guard to keep it that way.** A concept-content alignment pass across the other 6 shipped chapters uncovered that the duplication caught on Matrices was **systemic**: the worked examples had been seeded from their featured PYQ's exact numbers in the *majority* of bank-anchored concepts. Caught only by DB-diffing each `pyqExampleId`'s live text against its `authoredExample`.
  - **Scale.** ~80 worked examples / self-checks re-authored (hand-verified) across Sound, Probability, Statistics, 3D Geometry, Vectors, Indefinite Integration — plus the backlog the new lint then surfaced (Matrices & Determinants 14, 3D Geometry 7 the LLM audit had missed). Two finding classes: **(A) wrong/misleading** examples — fixed always (3D `plane-through-three-points` taught intercept-form not the cross-product method; `dcs-from-a-line` orphaned `l⁴+m⁴+n⁴`; `sphere-and-axes` garbled `r/√2`) — vs **(B) redundant-but-correct** (right concept + method, same numbers as the PYQ below it).
  - **Structural fixes.** Indefinite Integration: **subtopic-order swap** (Rational/Partial-Fractions before Trig II) so the Weierstrass / divide-by-cos² examples meet the arctan-quadratic machinery before they need it — clean reorder, no concept moves, no DB-tag cost. Vectors `scalar-projection` forward-method (uses the component dot product a subtopic early): fixed with an **inline dot-product gloss, NOT a concept move** — relocating it broke notes-lint because projection questions live in the Magnitude/Projection *taxonomy* subtopic and a featured PYQ must sit in the same one ([[notes-concept-content-alignment]]).
  - **PYQ retags (2).** Statistics `arithmetic-mean-raw`'s featured discard-problem → `mean-replacement-correction`; Vectors `triple-product-cyclic`'s unit-vector-sinθ PYQ → `cross-product-magnitude`.
  - **The durable guard — notes-lint check #5.** Fetches each PYQ's text and WARNs when a worked example re-uses its distinctive numbers (subscripts stripped — they're indices, not magnitudes; superscript powers kept). Non-blocking. Reversed the "deliberately not lintable" stance for the *number-heavy* slice; concept-illustration / forward-method / arithmetic-correctness remain the manual read-through (no gate checks math — a wrong fresh example is worse than a correct duplicate). It immediately found ~21 dups the manual+agent audit missed. CLAUDE.md "Notes editorial workflow" Step 1 + 4 updated (author the worked example FIRST then attach a *different* PYQ; prefer a second real PYQ over an invented one). See [[notes-concept-content-alignment]] + [[audit-scope-separation]].

- **2026-06-02 — NDA Maths Matrices & Determinants /notes chapter (7th) + concept-content alignment rule + CI/pre-push gap closed.** Built pedagogy-first; 170 q · 35 concepts · 100% tag coverage.
  - **The chapter.** 6 subtopics (matrix-operations · special-matrices · determinants-evaluation-properties · special-determinants · cofactors-adjoint-inverse · linear-systems), 34 formula + 1 reference concept. Largest+hardest notes chapter (31% HARD). **Pedagogy-first manifested as a decision NOT to reshape** — the bank's 6 subtopics already tracked the teaching spine, so reordered via `subtopicOrder` with no Phase-D (contrast 3D Geometry, which needed a split). **Diagram-light** (3 static schematics) — the diagram decision rule predicting the right outcome for an algebraic chapter. 2 cross-subtopic passage sets co-located (S11→cofactors, S14→special-matrices).
  - **Concept-content alignment rule (the user caught it on a rendered page).** The "scalar multiplication" concept's worked example was actually matrix *multiplication* (taught later, didn't illustrate the concept); a sweep found the "evaluating-determinants" example row-reduced (taught 2 concepts later) AND duplicated its own featured PYQ. Both fixed. Codified the rule in the Notes editorial workflow (Step 1 + Step 4 manual read-through) + [[notes-concept-content-alignment]]: worked examples must teach THEIR concept using only techniques taught by then (forward-POINTERS in prose are fine; forward-METHOD is not); worked example must differ from the featured PYQ; retag PYQs whose dominant skill is a later concept (`66be5b2d` moved equality→multiplication). Semantic — deliberately not a notes-lint rule.
  - **CI ≠ pre-push.** CI red-failed on the Matrices push: `notes-index-helper.test.ts` used "Matrices & Determinants" as a *no-notes* example, which shipping the chapter flipped to true. **Pre-push runs only typecheck+lint+build; CI adds `npm test` + notes:lint + notes:latex** — so the test break passed pre-push and only CI caught it. Fixed the test (synthetic negative cases now) + added "run full `npm test` before ship" to workflow Step 4. See [[prepush-excludes-npm-test]].

- **2026-06-02 — NDA Maths 3D Geometry /notes chapter (6th chapter) + diagram decision-rule codified.** Shipped pedagogy-first in 2 commits; gates green (notes:lint, notes:latex, prepush, guide-nda-maths-taxonomy).
  - **The chapter.** 5 subtopics · 29 concepts (27 formula + 2 reference) on the teaching arc coordinates → direction cosines → line → plane → sphere, authored against the 89-q bank. **Phase-D split:** the bank lumped lines + planes into one 25-q subtopic; pedagogically distinct, so "Lines and Planes in 3D" was re-carved into "The Straight Line in 3D" (11 q) + "The Plane" (14 q) (NDA Maths 109 → 110 sub), and the nda-maths guide principle drill + CHAPTER_TABLE focus prose synced in the same change (the [[shipped-chapter-rename-downstream-sync]] discipline). 89 `question_concept_tags` inserted (100% coverage, all 12 passage sets co-located).
  - **Diagrams — first chapter to start static, then upgraded.** Initial ship leaned static-2D on a chapter that's the most spatial in the bank; a follow-up commit converted octants / line-plane / plane-normal to **drag-to-rotate** (the Vectors `makeProject(az, el)` turntable) and added 2 new rotatable figures (angle-between-lines, sphere centre/radius), keeping sphere↔plane tangency a static cross-section (clearer in 2-D). 6 new SVGs total → 51 bank-wide.
  - **The rule that came out of it (answer to "should diagrams be default-on?"): NO — selective by decision rule, not mandatory.** A diagram on a non-spatial concept is decorative slop + a needless client-island; real hit-rate is ~40–60% on visual chapters, ~0% on recall chapters. Codified the 4-step rule in the Notes editorial workflow (Step 1) + [[notes-diagram-decision-rule]]: spatial? → 3-D? rotate : 2-D static; cap to load-bearing concepts. The miss this session (static-first on a 3-D chapter) is exactly what the rule now prevents.

Now the drill link carries `from`/`fromLabel`; a floating `BackToNotes` pill (bottom-LEFT, opposite the CartPill) returns them to the **exact concept anchor** (`…/subtopic#concept-slug`, lands via the card's existing `id={slug}` + `scroll-mt-20`). Captured into sessionStorage (`qb:returnTo:v1`) so it **survives pagination + filtering** (those navigations drop the param). Open-redirect guarded by `isSafeReturnPath` (only `/notes/` + `/guide/`). New: `src/lib/browse/returnTo.ts` (pure, TDD `tests/return-to.test.ts`, 16 tests) + `src/app/browse/BackToNotes.tsx` (client). Wired: `buildBrowseUrl` (+`from`/`fromLabel`), `ConceptUnitCard` (+`backHref`/`backLabel` props), `NotesSubtopicPage` (threads per-concept + subtopic-wide targets), `browse/page.tsx` (mounts the pill). **Guides not yet wired** — the /browse pill infra supports any `from`, but each guide `BrowseLink` would need a one-line `from=` per call site (deferred).

Keeps the `<h1>`, one-line sub, and the 2-stat band. ~40% shorter on the unfiltered /browse. (Continues the "stale scaffolding decays" line — [[stale-scaffolding-decay]].)

Adding a chapter-keyed subject is a one-row change. Behaviour-preserving (the 41-test `question-resources.test.ts` stayed green).
